级只剩黑白两色。 级数太少时，缓慢变化的区域出现台阶状的假轮廓（false contouring）。 p.55 把两件事放在一起：只采样（块状但层次连续）、只量化（形状光滑但亮度成台阶）、两者都有。 L 级要用几位来存，存一幅图要多少空间，按下面两段算。"}]]}],["$","figure",null,{"id":"der-bits","className":"my-7 <span class="line" data-line="2"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">from</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> scipy.ndimage </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">import</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> gaussian_filter</span></span>

<span class="line" data-line="4"><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># ---- 采样定理：看起来的频率 ----</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="5"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">def</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> alias_frequency</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(u, fs):</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="6"><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">    """频率 u 的正弦以 fs 采样后看起来的频率（折叠到 [0, fs/2]）"""</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="7"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">    return</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> abs</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(u </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> fs </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.round(u </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> fs))</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="8"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="9"><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">print</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(alias_frequency(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">10</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">))   </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 3.0  fs > 2u，不混叠</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="10"><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">print</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(alias_frequency(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">4</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">))    </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 1.0  fs &#x3C; 2u，3 Hz 看起来像 1 Hz</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="11"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="12"><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># fs = 2u 且相位为 0：所有样本都落在过零点</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="13"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">u, fs </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 2.0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">4.0</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="14"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">t </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.arange(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">3</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">1</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> /</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> fs)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="15"><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">print</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(np.allclose(np.sin(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> *</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.pi </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> u </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> t), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">))  </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># True：和一条直线分不开</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="16"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="17"><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># ---- 二维：同心环测试图，下采样前加不加低通 ----</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="18"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">N_src, N </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 512</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">64</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="19"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">y, x </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.mgrid[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">:N_src, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">:N_src] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> N_src          </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 行 = y，列 = x</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="20"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">alpha </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 128</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> *</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.pi </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 0.3</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="21"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">f </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 0.5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> +</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 0.5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> *</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.cos(alpha </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> ((x </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 0.5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">**</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> +</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (y </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 0.5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">**</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">))</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="22"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="23"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">s </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> N_src </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">//</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> N                                       </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 每个目标像素覆盖 s×s 个源像素</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="24"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">naive </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> f[s </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">//</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">::s, s </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">//</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">::s]                      </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 点采样：隔 s 个取一个</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="25"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">smooth </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> gaussian_filter(f, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">sigma</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0.7</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> *</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> s)[s </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">//</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">::s, s </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">//</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">::s]   </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 先低通再采样</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="26"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="27"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">rho_star </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.pi </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">*</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> N </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> *</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> alpha)                   </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 从这个半径往外开始混叠</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="28"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">yy, xx </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> (np.mgrid[</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">:N, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">:N] </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 0.5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">/</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> N              </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 目标像素中心</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="29"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">r </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.hypot(xx </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 0.5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, yy </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 0.5</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>

<span class="line" data-line="36"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">def</span><span style="--shiki-light:#6F42C1;--shiki-dark:#B392F0"> quantize</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(img, L):</span></span>

95:["$","div",null,{"className":"prose-vml mt-3","children":["$","p",null,{"children":[["$","strong",null,{"children":"HSV"}],"（p.50）按人描述颜色的方式来组织：色相 H（hue，是红是黄是蓝，用 0°–360° 的角度表示）、 饱和度 S（saturation，颜色有多「纯」，0 是灰，1 是纯色）、明度 V（value，有多亮）。 它的好处是光照变化主要落在 V 上：把一个颜色三个通道都乘以 0.5，H 和 S 完全不变，只有 V 减半。 所以按颜色分割物体时，常在 HSV 里只看 H。"]}]}]

<span class="line" data-line="23"><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">print</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(rgb_to_lab([</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">255</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">]).round(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 0.0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)        </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># [53.24 80.09 67.2 ]</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="24"><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">print</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(rgb_to_lab([</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">255</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">255</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">255</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">]).round(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">+</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 0.0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)    </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># [100.   0.   0.]</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="25"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="26"><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 两对颜色，RGB 距离都是 40</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="27"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">pairs </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> [([</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">255</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">255</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">], [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">255</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">255</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">40</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">]),            </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 亮黄 vs 稍微偏白的亮黄</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="28"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">         ([</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">], [</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">40</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">0</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">])]                    </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 黑 vs 很暗的绿</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="29"><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">for</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> A, B </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">in</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> pairs:</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="30"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    d_rgb </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.linalg.norm(np.subtract(A, B))</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="31"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">    d_lab </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> np.linalg.norm(rgb_to_lab(A) </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">-</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> rgb_to_lab(B))    </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># ΔE*ab（CIE76）</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="32"><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">    print</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">f</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">"RGB 距离 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">d_rgb</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:.1f</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">   ΔE*ab </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">{</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">d_lab</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">:.1f</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">}</span><span style="--shiki-light:#032F62;--shiki-dark:#9ECBFF">"</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)        </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 40.0 / 4.6，然后 40.0 / 32.6</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="33"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="34"><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># HSV：调暗一半，H 和 S 不变，V 减半</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="35"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">h1, s1, v1 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> colorsys.rgb_to_hsv(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">200</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> /</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 255</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">120</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> /</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 255</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">40</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> /</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 255</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="36"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">h2, s2, v2 </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> colorsys.rgb_to_hsv(</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">100</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> /</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 255</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">60</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> /</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 255</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">20</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583"> /</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF"> 255</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>

<span class="line" data-line="4"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">h, w </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> img.shape[:</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">2</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">]                           </span><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># shape 是 (行数, 列数, 通道)，即 (M, N, 3)</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="5"></span>
<span class="line" data-line="6"><span style="--shiki-light:#6A737D;--shiki-dark:#6A737D"># 下采样：INTER_NEAREST 近似点采样；INTER_AREA 对每个目标像素取面积平均，能减轻混叠</span></span>
<span class="line" data-line="7"><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">small_nearest </span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8"> cv2.resize(img, (</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">64</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">, </span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">64</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">), </span><span style="--shiki-light:#E36209;--shiki-dark:#FFAB70">interpolation</span><span style="--shiki-light:#D73A49;--shiki-dark:#F97583">=</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">cv2.</span><span style="--shiki-light:#005CC5;--shiki-dark:#79B8FF">INTER_NEAREST</span><span style="--shiki-light:#24292E;--shiki-dark:#E1E4E8">)</span></span>
